- 2026-08-27 — W1 DESIGN RULED: APPROVED AS AMENDED (owner, direct).
  Spec of record: docs/notes/2026-08-m6-w1-ipqp-spec.md (v2). The
  path to the ruling, for the record: the settler's first pass
  mirrored the SSN kIndefinite hand-off; the second pass checked it
  against production practice (Ipopt/Knitro/SNOPT/WORHP/acados —
  nobody running an IP QP inside SQP hands off on indefiniteness)
  and moved to convexify-first; the third pass corrected that to
  INERTIA-GATED exactness (acados' always-convexify costs local
  convergence; the gate is what Ipopt has) and wrongly credited
  IP-PMM's proximal term with proximal-point convergence on nonconvex
  QPs. The SQP lane's deep review (tycho-sqp-73) then found two
  things that would have shipped: (1) a STRUCTURAL COLLISION with
  the ruled three-tier composition — routing near-converged barrier
  iterates to the walk, when the ruling says the finish is the
  stable-face refinement "never re-entering the one-row walk" (and
  crash_basis_seed takes only a QpProblem — the transfer was
  unbuildable as drafted); (2) a SADDLE EXPOSURE — with the proximal
  centre at the current iterate the step IS Ipopt's δ_w, the PPM
  story does not apply, and the inertia observed at convergence is
  that of H+ρI, so the tier would certify saddles exactly as SSN did
  before T4. Both adopted. The design of record: inertia gate first
  (right → exact Newton); inertia-demanded W-B regularization on a
  current-iterate anchor (explicitly NOT proximal-point), monotone
  per-solve ladder, REQUIRED final unregularized inertia read (+1
  factorization) with certificate downgrade; routing barrier→~1e-8
  → ratio-rule face threshold → refine_on_face → SSN warm-grade →
  walk COLD last; escapes fresh-per-major (bench/backoff withdrawn on
  the P7 census evidence), retire after K=3, early stall rule (W=5
  accepted steps; μ, residual, α conjuncts), infeasible-suspect
  two-conjunct mirror with evidence block; W3 FOLDED INTO W1
  (positivity + SAY shift + re-centering + μ0 clamp; warm-kill rule;
  base-currency zL/zU split); Ruiz inside the tier (composes with
  W0.2); ~1e-8 tail hand-off; the honest IP-PMM edge stated as the
  dual regularization δ (degenerate rows without LICQ) + IP warm
  start through the currency. REGISTERED: elastic-QP formulation as
  the QP-level infeasibility answer → W2 design input; R6 third-
  producer coverage; per-piece convexification needs a second seam
  view (AggregateEvalSeam flattens to one CSR arena) → M7; HS
  indefinite rows, perturbed-continuation, same-occasion PSIOPT
  re-read, LTO/threads context leg as acceptance cells; naming
  kIpm/IPQP.

- 2026-08-31 — **OWNER QUESTION Q-O2 (presented at T5 close): the §2.2
  ladder freezes nonconvex subproblems.** Plain language: when the QP's
  Hessian has a negative direction, the tier adds a shift `rho` to make the
  Newton system safe. Today the shift is chosen by a ladder that (1) is
  consulted only AFTER the same iteration has already lowered `rho` on the
  ordinary schedule — so a shift that was already sufficient (8 on the HS
  rows) is thrown away first; (2) jumps by ×100 from the lowered value, so
  it cannot land anywhere between 0.8 and 80 and overshoots the needed ≈ 2
  by 8–40×; and (3) is never allowed to come back down within the solve
  (§2.2 item 3, "the ladder is MONOTONE per solve" — an hven addition; the
  Wächter–Biegler Algorithm IC the spec cites by name starts each new trial
  BELOW the last value, at 1/3 of it, and escalates ×8). The result is an
  exact stationary point of the SHIFTED problem where nothing moves and the
  budget is burned: no Hock–Schittkowski indefinite row converges under the
  tier at W1 head, so acceptance cell A11's HS half is NOT MET (pinned as
  such, flips loudly when fixed). The convex F7 corpus is untouched by this
  — it never arms the ladder. Options: (A) amend §2.2 to Algorithm IC
  proper — read the inertia BEFORE the gated decrease; first trial after a
  previous shift = last_floor / 3, escalate ×8 (×100 only from zero); the
  monotone floor becomes "never below the smallest shift that was
  sufficient THIS iteration", i.e. re-searched downward once per iteration
  — a small T4 follow-on (T4b, ≈ S/M) before T9, replay-inert (F7 never
  arms the ladder), with A11's HS pin flipped to == 3 as its close gate;
  (B) keep §2.2 as written for W1, carry A11-HS NOT MET into T9/T10 as a
  measured limitation and fix in W2/M7 — cheaper now, but T9's battery and
  T10's A4 taxonomy would be measured on a tier that cannot solve nonconvex
  QPs; (C) something else the owner prefers. Settler recommendation: (A),
  before T9 — sequenced after T6 so the routing work is not blocked, i.e.
  T6 → T4b → T7 → T8 → T9 → T10. Q-O2 does not block T6.

  (settler refinement F' of amendment F, inside owner ruling Q-O3; tycho-sqp pre-read adopted):
  restoration starts at a CANDIDATE point iff the candidate's violation, measured on the
  restoration wrapper's OWN objective (caller-scale l1 — the slack sigma cancels the objective
  sigma, so "lower starting objective for the phase" IS the caller-scale comparison; the
  engine-scale h is the funnel's measure, which the phase does not minimize), is strictly below
  the entry's; otherwise at x. Candidates by request site: the elastic exhausted arm offers
  `clamp(x + p_elastic, model box)` (amendment F's point; a ZERO step is no offer — tycho TRAP 3);
  the judged-rejection floor and the funnel's kRestore sites offer the REJECTED TRIAL itself with
  its already-charged values bundle; the routed-failure floor offers nothing. A taken candidate
  builds `RestorationModel(bridge.model(), x_cand, ev_cand)` from a FULL caller-scale evaluation
  exactly as the entry does (tycho TRAP 1: the start is an (x, slack) PAIR; an x-only override on
  a wrapper built at x would be wrapper-infeasible and the sub-solve, `allow_restoration=false`,
  would exit kInfeasible from its own request); `RestorationModel` untouched. Measurement state is
  the OFFER'S SHAPE (fix1, Claude F1 = Codex 1): a null values bundle is the unmeasured elastic
  route, the only arm that may issue a model query; a measured trial whose bundle or h is
  non-finite refuses for FREE. Accounting on the evals_full/evals_values PARTITION

  bounded by the once-per-solve restoration cap); a taken trial is UPGRADED in place through
  `AggregateEvalSeam::refresh_derivatives` and RECLASSIFIED (−1 values, +1 full), never charged
  twice; a refused trial costs 0; the counter doc carries the exception. Je/Ji finiteness screened
  before any take (`NlpEval::all_finite` excludes the Jacobians the wrapper's sigmas read — tycho

  p_elastic; non-finite point; non-finite evaluation; guard failing; a throwing model propagates;

## THE CONTRACT, PINNED (the M6 exit criterion's text — readable standalone)

`certified_feasibility_fallback(engine, qp, ev, seed, evidence, overrides, opts, window, out,
row, fallback_report, verdict)` — a **bounded two-rung attempt ladder**, not a straight-to-elastic
jump and not a cold walk first. Rung A is `run_elastic_ladder` on the EVIDENCE arm
(`ElasticSeedSource{nullptr, &evidence}`) at the driver's finite window
`min(delta, opts_.qp.tr_radius)`; rung B is W1's cold walk, entered only on rung A's
non-`kOptimal` exit. There is no third rung: restoration is the driver's tier, not this
function's.

| # | rung A outcome | returned status | driver consequence | pins (all pass in BOTH configs) |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 0 | `evidence.fired == false` — rung A **not entered** | rung B's cold walk, **no report** | W1's behaviour exactly; nothing charged in the partition; the trace event is `kUnfired`, never `kRungB` | `SqpDriverCertifiedFallback.P5AnUnfiredEvidenceBlockNeverEntersRungAAtAll`; `SqpDriverElasticSeed.ADefaultConstructedEvidenceBlockIsW1sLadderExactly`; `SqpDriverElasticSeed.ABlockThatNeverFIREDIsW1sLadderWhateverItsFieldsSay`; at the driver `SqpDriverCertifiedFallback.TheDriverEmitsOneFallbackVerdictPerEscapeAndHS38sNeverFIRED` |
| 1 | slacks **closed** (`l1 <= feas_tol`) | `kOptimal`, multipliers **carried** (`elastic_project(carry = true)`), report attached | ordinary funnel judgment; the suspicion is **disproved** | `SqpDriverCertifiedFallback.P2ClosedSlacksDisproveTheSuspicionAndCarryTheMultipliers`; calibration `…T7TheDisprovedSuspicionCalibrationOverTheHsCorpusIsZERO` |
| 2 | slacks open but **`reduced`** or **`promises_f`** | `kOptimal`, multipliers **ZEROED** (`carry = false`), report attached | ordinary judgment on a relaxed step; the major is an **ELASTIC ROW** (`elastic_applied` true, `qp_minor_iters`/`qp_factorizations` read from the report), so SOC — gated on `!elastic_applied` — cannot run on it | `…P2bOpenButReducedSlacksReturnKOptimalWithMultipliersZEROED`; driver row `…ARungAOwnedMajorIsAnElasticRowOnTheDriversOwnHistory` |
| 3 | ladder **exhausted**, relaxation still open | `kInfeasible` **+ the report attached** — the only `kInfeasible` this function SYNTHESIZES | the escape branch CONSUMES the report in the ternary form; the history row and `enter_restoration()` run off the ladder that already ran; **no second ladder** (`++elastic_activations` lives inside `run_elastic_ladder`, so a re-run double-charges by construction) | `…P3AnExhaustedLadderCertifiesInfeasibleWithItsReportAttached`; `…P3TheEscapeBranchCONSUMESTheAttachedLadderRatherThanRerunningIt`; at the driver `…T7TheEscapeBranchTernaryCONSUMESAnAttachedReportAtTheDriver` (discriminated by `elastic_activations == 2`, not 3) |
| 4 | rung A **not `kOptimal`** — the REFUSAL | rung B's walk solution **verbatim**, **NO report** | `qs` carries the walk's own status into the failure routing; a refusal costs a radius shrink and a retry, never an abort. **No suspicion is ever promoted.** | `…P4ARefusedRungAReturnsTheColdWalkCounterForCounter`; `…P4OnAPrimedEngineCostsTheAnalysisRungADisplaces`; `…P4bTheCeilingDeclineIsGONEUnderThePlacementBound`; `…T3ARefusalWhoseWalkCertifiesInfeasibleCostsTwoActivations`; `…T7TheT3ARouteIsREACHABLEAtTheDriverAndCostsOneExtraLadder` |

### The amendments folded into that table, each attributed

* **T1 (extraction, `ElasticLadderReport`).** Both halves of the driver's elastic block — the rung
  LOOP and the usability VERDICT — live in one function, so there is **one ladder implementation
  and one judge**, and "which ladder judged this" is a typed value rather than an inference.
  `closed`/`reduced` are guarded under `kOptimal` (tycho-sqp: on a non-`kOptimal` rung `s_final`
  is empty, `slack_l1 == 0`, and rows 1–2 would read vacuously true). Pins: the elastic fixture
  family unchanged (`SqpDriverElastic.*`), plus
  `SqpDriverGlobalization.SubproblemInfeasibilityEndsTheSolveThroughTheElasticTier`.
* **T2 (evidence-informed placement + a WORKING-SET seed; amendment H / Q-S4 with `c = 1`).**
  `rho_0 = min(kElasticRhoMax, max(kElasticRhoInit, dual_norm_start))` computed ONCE and fed to
  both `build_elastic_subproblem` and the ladder's local `rho`; the seed is a WORKING SET, never a
  raw point (amendment B — `seed.x` is the engine's window CENTRE and is zeroed on every rung, so
  a raw point cannot be a seed no matter what surface is added). Bounds are classified against the
  EFFECTIVE window-clamped box. Degrade: `!fired`, missing/mis-sized duals or `mu == 0` ⇒
  GEOMETRIC activity; empty `x` ⇒ W1's no-hint seed. Pins:
  `SqpDriverElasticSeed.TheFirstRungsPenaltyIsFlooredAtTodaysStartAndPlacedByTheEvidence`,
  `…TheWorkingSetSeedIsSolutionIdenticalAndEarnsItsMinorIterations`,
  `…TheWorkingSetIsClassifiedAgainstTheWINDOWCLAMPEDBounds`,
  `…GeometricActivityEARNSItsMinorIterationsWhereTheGeometryIsThere`,
  `…TheDegradedPathIsSAFEOnFixturesWhereGeometryNamesNothing`,
  `…AnEvidenceNormAboveTheLaddersCeilingIsCAPPEDAtIt`; contract-level
  `SqpDriverCertifiedFallback.P1TheEvidencePlacesRungAsFirstRungBetweenTheFloorAndTheCeiling`.
* **T5 (the dual_mu-aware PLACEMENT BOUND, the FLOOR RETRY, and the ENTERED partition —
  owner-backed ruling recorded at T3; a DECLARED BREAK, spent).** The placement is
  `rho_0 = max(kElasticRhoInit, min(max(kElasticRhoInit, dual_norm_start),
  min(kElasticRhoMax / kElasticRhoFactor, kElasticRhoDualMuSafety / opts.qp.dual_mu)))` with
  `kElasticRhoDualMuSafety = 1e-2`; **the floor outranks both caps**. A declined rung A whose
  `rho_0 > kElasticRhoInit` is **retried once at the floor** before rung B, charged as the second
  activation it is. `rho_0_override` is validated at the boundary (NaN / non-positive throw).
  Pins: `…AnOverriddenPlacementIsVALIDATEDAndClampedIntoTheLaddersRange`,
  `…ADeclinedRungAAboveTheFloorIsRETRIEDThereExactlyOnce`,
  `…T7AnInfiniteOverridePlacesTheLadderAtItsCeiling` (the `+inf` legal clause through a literal
  infinity), and the ceiling flag's true arm at the driver
  `…T7TheCeilingFlagsTRUEArmOnADriverRowIsThePricedOne` (HS11's 1.784456e7 placement).
* **T6b (the walk's FALSE `kInfeasible` under dual regularization — a `kSchurBorder`-only
  artifact, fixed at the VERDICT SITE; a DECLARED BREAK).** Border mode realizes a pin as
  `add_border(pin_variable, −dual_mu)` and the solve satisfies the written bound value only to
  `O(mu·|y|)`; that SCATTER moves every row the pinned variable sits in, and the bordered loop's
  footprint is in PENALTY units while the verdict reads ROW units. The verdict-site loop (entered
  when the classifier says `kInfeasible` AND the candidate came off the bordered path AND
  `refine_steps > 0`) replays the incumbent against the unregularized bordered system, with
  CLOSED-OR-NOTHING adoption; the adopted point is what the classifier re-reads AND what a
  `kInfeasible` exit returns. Steps counted in `QpCounters::verdict_refine_steps`. Pins, all
  **MODE-PAIRED** (border verdict == refactorize verdict per cell):
  `QpEngineStructuralViolation.T6bAFeasibleElasticCopyIsNoLongerCertifiedInfeasible`,
  `…T6bTrueInfeasibilityStillReadsKInfeasibleInBothAlgebras`,
  `…T6bTheGuardContradictionsAreNotAbsorbedInEitherAlgebra`,
  `…T6bTheStiffFamilyClosesInBothAlgebras`,
  `…T6bTheInequalityBranchClosesWithItsResidueOnTheSafeSide`,
  `…T6bTheGENUINELYBlockedSiblingStaysKOptimalAndOPEN`,
  `…T6bTheMisfireCensusOverBothPopulationsAndBothAlgebras`,
  `SqpDriverElasticSeed.T6bAClimbingConsistentLadderClosesAtEveryRung`,
  `…T6bTheLadderAboveLambda1e6ClosesToo`, `…T6bTheUncontainedInBranchRouteNoLongerDeclines`,
  `…T6bCoversTheBandWhereThePlacementMarginIsUnachievable`,
  `…T6bTheBandsHIGHRungsRemainAPREEXISTINGResidue`; and T7's owed pins

  (Z-8) and `…T7TheFeasibleCensusHalfOverTheFULLDualMuSet` (Z-4).
* **T7 (the SYMMETRIC twin for the ELIMINATED path — a DECLARED contract change — and its
  provenance-keyed dispatch, a DECLARED BREAK).** `kRefactorize` had eight own (i) cases on the
  T6b families, one at the shipped `dual_mu`, where the equivalence ORACLE certified a feasible
  problem infeasible while border did not; `refine_eliminated_face_for_verdict` closes all eight.
  The dispatch keys on **the candidate's PROVENANCE** (`eqp.refine_steps > 0` is exactly the
  bordered-path witness), not on `opts_.ws_algebra` — the fix-round-1 correction, without which
  five routes that hand a bordered iteration to `eliminated_candidate` ran NEITHER twin and the
  DEFAULT mode read a feasible QP infeasible. The twin reuses the loop's own `kkt` under a
  captured working-set SIGNATURE with the **factor's `(session_id, epoch)` folded in**, so any
  re-factorization by any path invalidates the key structurally, and factorizes fresh (counted)
  only on a guard MISS. Pins:
  `QpEngineIndefinite.T7Fix2TheFaceKeyIsInvalidatedByAnyRefactorizationOfKkt`,
  `…T7Fix3ProbeInertiaLeavesTheKeyOnTheProbedSetOrDisarmed`,
  `…T7Fix2AFailedStartRepairDoesNotRefineThroughTheProbedFactorization`,
  `…T7Fix2DualMuZeroOnARankDeficientFaceDoesNotEscapeSolve`.
* **T4 (the RESTORATION SEED — mode-independent, owner ruling Q-O3; a DECLARED BREAK, unspent).**
  Restoration starts at a CANDIDATE point **iff the candidate's violation, measured on the
  restoration wrapper's OWN objective in CALLER scale, is strictly below the entry's**; otherwise
  at `x`. Offers by request site: the elastic exhausted arm offers `clamp(x + p_elastic, model
  box)` (a ZERO step is no offer); the judged-rejection floor and the funnel's `kRestore` sites
  offer the REJECTED TRIAL with its already-charged bundle; the routed-failure floor offers
  nothing. A taken candidate rebuilds `RestorationModel(bridge.model(), x_cand, ev_cand)` from a
  FULL caller-scale evaluation, because the start is an `(x, slack)` PAIR. Accounting rides the
  `evals_full`/`evals_values` PARTITION: +1 `evals_full` for the elastic guard query taken or
  refused, a taken trial UPGRADED in place and RECLASSIFIED (−1 values, +1 full), a refused trial
  free. Je/Ji finiteness is screened before any take. Every degrade sets the flag false and starts
  at `x` — never a throw. **Both offers are KEPT** (T7 ruling R2, reversing the settler's stated
  withdraw intent: T4's condition was "if T7's CORPUS CELL shows no elastic-site take" and no
  corpus cell exists, while T4 landed a config-stable scaled elastic-site take). Pins:
  `SqpDriverRestorationSeed.TheSeedIsNotGatedOnTheQpMode` (falsifiable mode-independence — the
  `if (qp_mode == kWalk)` mutant dies in both configs),
  `…TheExhaustedLadderSiteSeedsFromItsOwnStep`, `…TheTakenCandidateIsRecordedOnTheRequestingRow`,
  `…TheRadiusFloorSeedsFromTheRejectedTrial`, `…AWorseElasticCandidateIsRefusedOnTheSameModel`,
  `…AZeroElasticStepIsNotACandidate`, `…ANonFiniteEvaluationAtTheCandidateIsRefused`,
  `…AJacobianPoisonedCandidateIsRefusedNotTaken`, `…TheGuardAndThePhaseRunOnTheCallerScale`
  (the engine-scale mutant dies in both configs), `…TheSeedsEvaluationIsCountedExactlyOnce`;
  and `SqpDriverRestoration.ExhaustedElasticTierEntersRestorationUnderEveryMode`.

### The invariants that make it a contract, and their pins

| invariant | pin |
|---|---|
| **P1** evidence in ⇒ rung A ran at the placed `rho_0`, between the floor and the ceiling | `…P1TheEvidencePlacesRungAsFirstRungBetweenTheFloorAndTheCeiling` |
| **P6** the function never throws on any evidence CONTENT | `…P6NoEvidenceContentMakesTheFallbackThrow` |
| **P7** the VERDICT reads NOTHING from the evidence beyond `fired` (branch), `least_infeasible_x` + duals (seed) and `dual_norm_start` (placement) — pinned BY MUTATION, over a FIELD-COMPLETE output set | `…P7TheVerdictReadsNothingFromTheEvidenceBeyondItsThreeInputs` |
| the window is VALIDATED at both public entry points (`!(window >= 0)` throws; NaN caught, `+inf` legal) BEFORE anything runs | `…ANegativeOrNaNWindowIsREFUSEDAtBothPublicEntryPoints` |
| **the two counter identities** — `elastic_activations ==` walk-route `+ elastic_from_ipqp_escape`, and the ENTRY partition `disproved + relaxed + exhausted + rung_b == fired entries == elastic_from_ipqp_escape − elastic_floor_retries` | `…ThePartitionOverFallbackEntriesIsCOMPLETEAndSumsToTheFiredOnes`; and, from the trace side, `IpqpTrace.FallbackVerdictEventsReproduceThePartitionCountersWithoutAFilter` |
| one `fallback.verdict` trace event per ENTRY, `kUnfired` included | `…TheFallbackVerdictEventDescribesEVERYEntryIncludingTheUnfiredOne`; `…TheDriverEmitsOneFallbackVerdictPerEscapeAndHS38sNeverFIRED` |
| the two-rung ladder's own measured cost: on the driver's PRIMED engine a refusal pays **+1 `symbolic_analyses`** rung A displaced, and the major FOLLOWING a rung-A-owned one pays one analysis | `…P4OnAPrimedEngineCostsTheAnalysisRungADisplaces`; `…T7TheDisplacedPatternCostsONEAnalysisOnTheFollowingMajor` |
| F-1, the same inconsistent linearization in all three modes, with the route census per mode | `…T7F1TheSameInconsistentLinearizationInAllThreeModes` |
| the verdict-refine cost ceiling on the fixture set (15 steps; the scale reading is 0 over 1281 cells) | `…T7TheVerdictRefineCostAtScaleIsFIFTEENStepsOnItsWorstFixture` |

**T8's independent re-measurement of the two identities**: the kIpm counter leg
(`docs/notes/data/2026-09-m6-w2-acceptance/kipm-counters-{release,debug}.csv`) checks both on
EVERY row of every W2 fixture family at kIpm — the 27 HS models, F-1 in all three modes, the
driver-level exhaustion cell, the T3-A route and P4's refusal — in BOTH configs: **0 identity
failures.** The HS corpus at kIpm reproduces T7's calibration exactly (9 entries, 6 fired, 0
disproved, 6 relaxed, 0 exhausted, 0 rung-B).

Two gaps the PLAN itself flagged and W2 answers with a measurement rather than a change:

* **§6's "Corpus cell — none reaches either machine."** T7 swept 1200 F7 cells (N × p × taxonomy ×
  three arms, through the corpus's OWN generators) and found **no** configuration that reaches the
  elastic tier in any mode; recorded at `docs/notes/data/2026-09-m6-w2-t7-corpus-sweep/` with a
  byte-identical reproducer. **T7 is therefore NOT a gate and F-1..F-4 stand as W2's coverage**,
  exactly as W1 reported for its §6.2 stall test. No detector was moved, no window shortened, no
  bench column added — the 76-column schema is untouched by the whole window.
* **§6's F-3b (the end-to-end FALSE suspicion).** Not reached, and reported as such rather than
  provoked: over the HS corpus at kIpm the detector fires six times and is disproved zero times,
  and on the F7 corpus it never fires at all. F-3a (the unit-level contract cell) carries the
  calibration claim, which is why it does not depend on anything being reachable end-to-end.

## THE CORRECTION RECORD — the settler's and the lane's errors this window

Recorded in one place because the readiness view should be able to weigh them.

| # | the error | who caught it, and how |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | **The T6b brief's "fix direction" was WRONG.** The settler's brief told the implementer to test the UNEXPLAINED residual `u = resid − dual_mu·λ`. The regularized identity holds on inconsistent rows too, so that predicate makes `kInfeasible` UNREACHABLE. | **tycho-sqp, Addendum 3, PRE-implementation** — before a line was written. The brief's own pin 2 fails by construction under it. |
| 2 | **The T7 WITHDRAW intent, reversed.** The settler had stated to the owner an intent to withdraw T4's elastic-site restoration offer. | **T7's own measurement plus a re-read of T4's stated condition** (settler ruling R2): the condition was "if T7's CORPUS CELL shows no elastic-site take", no corpus cell exists, and T4 had landed a config-stable scaled elastic-site take that withdrawal would have deleted. |
| 3 | **The R6 catch ruling, reversed.** At T7 fix round 2 the settler accepted a MISS-branch catch that declined a singular factorization as the twin's own degradation class. | **The scoped re-review (Opus, the Codex substitute), fix-2 item Y-1**: `FactorizeOutcome::Status` has no singular status to discriminate on, so the catch's entire reachable domain on MKL was genuine backend faults silently converted to a decline — CLAUDE.md §4's never-swallow class, and asymmetric with the border twin's predicate-guarded catch. The catch was REMOVED at fix round 3. |
| 4 | **T3's F2 mechanism prose (tycho-owned).** The ceiling decline was characterized as a dual-regularization-FOOTPRINT effect with the law `residual ∝ dual_mu · rho`. | **Retracted at T5**: at a closed slack the footprint is `dual_mu·λ*` with `λ*` the ORIGINAL row multiplier and therefore rho-INDEPENDENT. The 3.2e-8 accuracy NUMBER was kept and the ATTRIBUTION withdrawn (tycho-sqp's own ruling); T6b then confirmed the corrected (scatter) form. |
| 5 | **Addendum 3's absorbency rule (tycho-owned).** The data-derived `K·μ²·(‖g+Hx‖∞/‖a_j‖₁)` absorbency landed at `8b6d0ac`. | **The Opus co-review (Codex substitute), F1–F4**: it hides genuine contradictions at the shipped `mu`, is defeated by one unrelated pinned variable because the price scale is a GLOBAL norm, destroys detection at `mu >= 1e-4` (the census had sampled `mu <= 1e-6` only), and — decisively — **the defect is `kSchurBorder`-ONLY**, confirmed by tycho on every cell. The absorbency was REVERTED byte-identically and the fix re-shaped to the verdict site. |
| 6 | **T7 fix round 1's `dual_mu = 0` claim (tycho-owned).** Their review argued the twin throws at `dual_mu = 0` on a rank-deficient face. | **Owned by tycho at their fix-2 second look**: true of the MATRIX, unreachable on MKL — Pardiso PERTURBS an exactly singular KKT and returns success (36 twin entries, 0 declines, 0 throws). |
| 7 | **R6's premise (tycho-owned).** R6 was tycho's recommendation, made on that wrong premise, and the settler adopted it. | **Recorded by tycho themselves** (§6 of their fix-2 review). Their stated lesson: *a decline branch needs a PREDICATE that identifies the degradation, never an exception class alone.* |
| 8 | **The epoch-conjunct cell claim (tycho-owned, carried into their §2 unverified).** The fix-2 report claimed the survivor `QpEngineIndefinite.UnboundedIndefiniteReportsNumericalError` was caught ONLY by the epoch conjunct. | **The scoped re-review, Y-2**: that cell never probes (`epoch_moved = 0`, twin entries 0). R5 alone closes every stale cell in the tree; the epoch conjunct is a STRUCTURAL guarantee, pinned by a unit fixture instead. |

**THE CODEX SUBSTITUTION, DECLARED.** Codex co-reviewed T1, T2 and T3 and performed their scoped
re-reviews. From **T4's scoped re-review onward the Codex usage quota was exhausted** (it returns
**2026-09-06 22:44**), and every Codex slot from that point — T4's scoped re-review, and both the
co-review and the scoped re-review of T5, T6b and T7 — was filled by an **independent, freshly
launched Opus read-only reviewer with the same scope and the same recompute rule**, declared in
each task's entry and in each review artifact under `.superpowers/`. The substitution is not a
formality: the substitute's T6b F1–F4 is the finding that re-shaped that task, and its T7 Y-1 is
the finding that removed the MISS-branch catch. Three of the window's eight recorded corrections
were caught by it. Codex's own three tasks are unaffected; the standing policy (Codex co-reviews
every task and owns scoped re-reviews) resumes when the quota does.

**Harness notes** (process, not product): the T2 implementer was killed by a harness restart after
its three commits and a Sonnet finisher wrote the report and evidence; the T3 fix-round
implementer hit the account session limit once; the T4 implementer was stopped and resumed from
the terminal once mid-leg; an Opus reviewer hit the session limit once and was relaunched. A
box-sharing collision during T3's replay (a paused lane's orphaned ninja held the lock fd for
2 h 11 min) produced the announce-before-every-solo-leg protocol and the `fuser` verification rule
now in force; both held for the whole of T8.

## WHERE THE WINDOW LEAVES THE PRODUCT

**The M6 exit criterion — "feasibility mode shipped with its refusal/fallback contract pinned" —
is MET**: the contract table above is landed, every row names the pins that hold it, and all of
them pass in both configs. Under kIpm the tier's infeasible-suspect escape now reaches the
elastic tier directly, with its evidence placing the first rung and its working set seeding it,
and reaches the restoration phase through a measured-better start point; the refusal path costs
exactly one extra declined solve relative to W1 and is pinned counter-for-counter; and the walk's
false `kInfeasible` under dual regularization — found while pinning that contract — is gone in
both algebras.

**What did NOT close, and is said plainly.** W2 has **no corpus coverage**: 1200 F7 cells reach
none of it, so F-1..F-4 and the driver fixtures are the whole of the coverage, and the detector's
false-fire rate at scale is UNMEASURED. The E1 proxy caveat did not retire. The tier-solved
elastic rung, the whole-solve bordered stopping rule and the ill-scaled driver-level family are
M7's. The coverage read (informational, MKL/Linux, instrument tree) is **82.74% lines overall**
against M5's 79.2%, and **`src/drivers` 77.94%** against M5's 67.8% — and the split says the
remaining driver gap is **not** in the SQP driver (`sqp_driver.cpp` 94.54%, `sqp_options.cpp`
100%, `src/qp/` 94.38%) but in the IPM driver's four TUs (66.26%, of which printing 15.72% and
settings 39.88%). **Apple/Accelerate: UNOBSERVED throughout.**

W2 CLOSE REVIEW: tycho-sqp PASS, nothing to ask (SIGNOFF W2-T8-SQP-REVIEW-FINAL) — recomputed from
the committed bytes: five replay comparisons 0/75 on 27 cells (stamps 0dda3dbcd027 / 4877bec876ad,
neither dirty), a4 0/65×29, both counter identities by their own script on every row of both kIpm
CSVs (HS 9/6/0/6/0/0 both configs), all 71 cited test names exist, a fresh 10a3dac worktree with
probes A–D 1113/1113 and 206 probe lines byte-identical to their T7 fix3 run; correction entry 5's
attribution confirmed by its owner (the absorbency shape endorsement in Addendum 3 was theirs); one
added clause on entry 4: the T3 F2 "×10 per decade" law was theirs and the table already read ×100.
The git stamp on the artefacts reads 4877bec876ad (the docs-only commit over the code head 10a3dac)

- 2026-09-11 — **Owner ruling: the `jet.h` question (from the T8.9 close) — hven provides THREAD-SAFE SOLVES, not a
  parallel-solve facility.** `detail/interior/jet.h` STAYS DELETED. Its map half (hand N problems to worker threads,
  collect results) is orchestration and belongs to the consumer — tycho keeps or reimplements its `Jet` map at the
  flip; its pin half (a thread-local MKL thread count that leaves the process-wide setting alone) is already in the
  library since T8.8 as `MklThreadScope` applied around every backend call under `common.threads`, so the
  replacement tycho needs is the option, not a header. **REGISTERED FOR W6 (quality floor):** the concurrency
  contract, stated and pinned — N solver instances on N threads at `threads = 1` produce results bit-identical to
